       [tma_branch_resteer > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_latency > 0.15 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_branch_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks +
        tma_unknown_branches]
       [tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_cisc
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to the microcode sequencer (MS)]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.CISC@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_cisc > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_decode
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to decode stalls]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.DECODE@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_decode > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Divider unit was
        active]
       [cpu_core@ARITH.DIV_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_dram_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled on accesses to
        external memory (DRAM) by loads]
       [cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L3_MISS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_dsb
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to DSB (decoded uop cache) fetch pipeline]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_dsb > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_dsb_switches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        switches from DSB to MITE pipelines]
       [cpu_core@DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dsb_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_fast_nuke
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to a machine clear that does not require the use of microcode,
        classified as a fast nuke,due to memory ordering,memory disambiguation
        and memory renaming]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_BAD_SPECULATION.FASTNUKE@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_fast_nuke > 0.05 & (tma_machine_clears > 0.05 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]
  tma_few_uops_instructions
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        instructions that that are decoder into two or more uops]
       [max(0,tma_heavy_operations - tma_microcode_sequencer)]
       [tma_few_uops_instructions > 0.05 & tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_fp_arith
       [This metric represents overall arithmetic floating-point (FP)
        operations fraction the CPU has executed (retired)]
       [tma_x87_use + tma_fp_scalar + tma_fp_vector]
       [tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_fused_instructions
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        fused instructions -- where one uop can represent multiple contiguous
        instructions]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.MACRO_FUSED@ /
        (tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fused_instructions > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_int_operations
       [This metric represents overall Integer (Int) select operations
        fraction the CPU has executed (retired)]
       [tma_int_vector_128b + tma_int_vector_256b]
       [tma_int_operations > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_TAG.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_l1_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled without loads
        missing the L1 Data (L1D) cache]
       [max((cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks,0)]
       [tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_MISS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to loads
        accesses to L3 cache or contended with a sibling Core]
       [(cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_MISS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L3_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_lcp
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU was stalled due to
        Length Changing Prefixes (LCPs)]
       [cpu_core@DECODE.LCP@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lcp > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_lsd
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to LSD (Loop Stream Detector) unit]
       [(cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_ACTIVE@ - cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_lsd > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_mem_scheduler
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to memory reservation stalls in which a scheduler is not able to
        accept uops]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_BE_BOUND.MEM_SCHEDULER@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_mem_scheduler > 0.1 & (tma_resource_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.1)]
  tma_memory_operations
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        memory operations -- uops for memory load or store accesses]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@MEM_UOP_RETIRED.ANY@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_memory_operations > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_microcode_sequencer
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was retiring uops
        fetched by the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 & tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_mite
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the MITE pipeline (the legacy decode pipeline)]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_ms
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit - see
        Microcode_Sequencer node for details]
       [max(cpu_core@IDQ.MS_CYCLES_ANY@,cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@)) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_ms > 0.05 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_ms_switches
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles when the CPU was stalled
        due to switches of uop delivery to the Microcode Sequencer (MS)]
       [3 * cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1\,edge@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ms_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_non_fused_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        branch instructions that were not fused]
       [tma_light_operations * (cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ -
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.MACRO_FUSED@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_non_fused_branches > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_non_mem_scheduler
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to IEC or FPC RAT stalls,which can be due to FIQ or IEC
        reservation stalls in which the integer,floating point or SIMD
        scheduler is not able to accept uops]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_BE_BOUND.NON_MEM_SCHEDULER@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_non_mem_scheduler > 0.1 & (tma_resource_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.1)]
  tma_nuke
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to a machine clear that requires the use of microcode (slow nuke)]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_BAD_SPECULATION.NUKE@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_nuke > 0.05 & (tma_machine_clears > 0.05 & tma_bad_speculation >
        0.15)]
  tma_other_fb
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to other common frontend stalls not categorized]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.OTHER@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_other_fb > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.2)]
  tma_other_light_ops
       [This metric represents the remaining light uops fraction the CPU has
        executed - remaining means not covered by other sibling nodes]
       [max(0,tma_light_operations - (tma_fp_arith + tma_int_operations +
        tma_memory_operations + tma_fused_instructions +
        tma_non_fused_branches))]
       [tma_other_light_ops > 0.3 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_other_mispredicts
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        other cases of misprediction (non-retired x86 branches or other types)]
       [max(tma_branch_mispredicts * (1 -
        cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ /
        (cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEARS_COUNT@ - cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@)),
        0.0001)]
       [tma_other_mispredicts > 0.05 & (tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]
  tma_other_nukes
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Nukes (Machine Clears) not related to memory ordering]
       [max(tma_machine_clears * (1 - cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.MEMORY_ORDERING@
        / cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@),0.0001)]
       [tma_other_nukes > 0.05 & (tma_machine_clears > 0.1 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]
  tma_ports_utilization
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU performance was
        potentially limited due to Core computation issues (non
        divider-related)]
       [((tma_ports_utilized_0 * tma_info_thread_clks +
        (cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.1_PORTS_UTIL@ + tma_retiring *
        cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.2_3_PORTS_UTIL@)) / tma_info_thread_clks if
        cpu_core@ARITH.DIV_ACTIVE@ < cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_TOTAL@ -
        cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_LOADS@ else
        (cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.1_PORTS_UTIL@ + tma_retiring *
        cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.2_3_PORTS_UTIL@) / tma_info_thread_clks)]
       [tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_predecode
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to wrong predecodes]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.PREDECODE@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_predecode > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_register
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to the physical register file unable to accept an entry (marble
        stalls)]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_BE_BOUND.REGISTER@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_register > 0.1 & (tma_resource_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.1)]
  tma_reorder_buffer
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to the reorder buffer being full (ROB stalls)]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_BE_BOUND.REORDER_BUFFER@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_reorder_buffer > 0.1 & (tma_resource_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.1)]
  tma_serialization
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to scoreboards from the instruction queue (IQ),jump execution unit
        (JEU),or microcode sequencer (MS)]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_BE_BOUND.SERIALIZATION@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_serialization > 0.1 & (tma_resource_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.1)]
  tma_serializing_operation
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU issue-pipeline was
        stalled due to serializing operations]
       [cpu_core@RESOURCE_STALLS.SCOREBOARD@ / tma_info_thread_clks +
        tma_c02_wait]
       [tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_store_bound
       [This metric estimates how often CPU was stalled due to RFO store
        memory accesses; RFO store issue a read-for-ownership request before
        the write]
       [cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_STORES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]

tma_L4_group: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 4]
  tma_assists
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops delivered
        by the Microcode_Sequencer as a result of Assists]
       [78 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.ANY@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_assists > 0.1 & (tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 &
        tma_heavy_operations > 0.1)]
  tma_c01_wait
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due
        staying in C0.1 power-performance optimized state (Faster wakeup time;
        Smaller power savings)]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.C01@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_c01_wait > 0.05 & (tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_c02_wait
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due
        staying in C0.2 power-performance optimized state (Slower wakeup time;
        Larger power savings)]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.C02@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_c02_wait > 0.05 & (tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_cisc
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU retired uops
        originated from CISC (complex instruction set computer) instruction]
       [max(0,tma_microcode_sequencer - tma_assists)]
       [tma_cisc > 0.1 & (tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 &
        tma_heavy_operations > 0.1)]
  tma_clears_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Machine Clears]
       [(1 - tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation) *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_clears_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_l2_hit
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that hit in the L2 cache]
       [max(0,tma_icache_misses - tma_code_l2_miss)]
       [tma_code_l2_hit > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_l2_miss
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that miss in the L2 cache]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_CODE_RD@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_l2_miss > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the (first
        level) ITLB was missed by instructions fetches,that later on hit in
        second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [max(0,tma_itlb_misses - tma_code_stlb_miss)]
       [tma_code_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the Second-level
        TLB (STLB) was missed by instruction fetches,performing a hardware
        page walk]
       [cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(25 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ *
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) + 24 *
        tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS@) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_data_sharing
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to data-sharing accesses]
       [24 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_NO_FWD@ +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ * (1 -
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_data_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_decoder0_alone
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where decoder-0 was the only
        active decoder]
       [(cpu_core@INST_DECODED.DECODERS\,cmask\=1@ -
        cpu_core@INST_DECODED.DECODERS\,cmask\=2@) / tma_info_core_core_clks /
        2]
       [tma_decoder0_alone > 0.1 & (tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth >
        0.2)]
  tma_dtlb_load
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the Data
        TLB (DTLB) was missed by load accesses]
       [min(7 * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.STLB_HIT\,cmask\=1@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@,
        max(cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_MEM_ANY@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_L1D_MISS@,0)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_dtlb_store
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles spent handling
        first-level data TLB store misses]
       [(7 * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.STLB_HIT\,cmask\=1@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [28 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_fb_full
       [This metric does a *rough estimation* of how often L1D Fill Buffer
        unavailability limited additional L1D miss memory access requests to
        proceed]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.FB_FULL@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fb_full > 0.3]
  tma_fp_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Floating-Point
        Divider unit was active]
       [cpu_core@ARITH.FPDIV_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fp_divider > 0.2 & (tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_fp_scalar
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) scalar uops
        fraction the CPU has retired]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_scalar > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_fp_vector
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) vector uops
        fraction the CPU has retired aggregated across all vector widths]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.VECTOR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_int_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Integer Divider
        unit was active]
       [tma_divider - tma_fp_divider]
       [tma_int_divider > 0.2 & (tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_int_vector_128b
       [This metric represents 128-bit vector Integer ADD/SUB/SAD or VNNI
        (Vector Neural Network Instructions) uops fraction the CPU has retired]
       [(cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.ADD_128@ +
        cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.VNNI_128@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_int_vector_128b > 0.1 & (tma_int_operations > 0.1 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]
  tma_int_vector_256b
       [This metric represents 256-bit vector Integer ADD/SUB/SAD/MUL or VNNI
        (Vector Neural Network Instructions) uops fraction the CPU has retired]
       [(cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.ADD_256@ + cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.MUL_256@
        + cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.VNNI_256@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_int_vector_256b > 0.1 & (tma_int_operations > 0.1 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]
  tma_l1_latency_dependency
       [This metric([SKL+] roughly; [LNL]) estimates fraction of cycles with
        demand load accesses that hit the L1D cache]
       [min(2 * (cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ -
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@) * 20 / 100,
        max(cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_MEM_ANY@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_L1D_MISS@,0)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l1_latency_dependency > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_l2_hit_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L2 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L2 latency
        limited)]
       [3 * tma_info_system_core_frequency * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L2_HIT@
        * (1 + cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@ / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_hit_latency > 0.05 & (tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_l3_hit_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L3 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L3 latency
        limited)]
       [9 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L3_HIT@ * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_hit_latency > 0.1 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_lock_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling cache
        misses due to lock operations]
       [(16 * max(0,cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.ALL_RFO@) + cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@ * (10 *
        cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT@ + min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO@))) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lock_latency > 0.2 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2
        & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=4@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_memory_fence
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        LFENCE Instructions]
       [13 * cpu_core@MISC2_RETIRED.LFENCE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_memory_fence > 0.05 & (tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mispredicts_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Branch Misprediction at execution stage]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mispredicts_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_nop_instructions
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        NOP (no op) instructions]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.NOP@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_nop_instructions > 0.1 & (tma_other_light_ops > 0.3 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]
  tma_ports_utilized_0
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed no uops on any
        execution port (Logical Processor cycles since ICL,Physical Core
        cycles otherwise)]
       [(cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.EXE_BOUND_0_PORTS@ +
        max(cpu_core@RS.EMPTY_RESOURCE@ - cpu_core@RESOURCE_STALLS.SCOREBOARD@,
        0)) / tma_info_thread_clks * (cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_TOTAL@ -
        cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_LOADS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_0 > 0.2 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_1
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the CPU executed total
        of 1 uop per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.1_PORTS_UTIL@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_1 > 0.2 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_2
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 2 uops
        per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles since ICL,
        Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.2_PORTS_UTIL@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_2 > 0.15 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_3m
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 3 or
        more uops per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.CYCLES_GE_3@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_3m > 0.4 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_shuffles_256b
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        Shuffle operations of 256-bit vector size (FP or Integer)]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.SHUFFLES@ /
        (tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_shuffles_256b > 0.1 & (tma_other_light_ops > 0.3 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]
  tma_slow_pause
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        PAUSE Instructions]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.PAUSE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_slow_pause > 0.05 & (tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_split_loads
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles handling memory load split
        accesses - load that cross 64-byte cache line boundary]
       [tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency * cpu_core@LD_BLOCKS.NO_SR@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_split_loads > 0.3]
  tma_split_stores
       [This metric represents rate of split store accesses]
       [cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.SPLIT_STORES@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_split_stores > 0.2 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(cpu_core@XQ.FULL_CYCLES@ + cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.L2_STALLS@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_fwd_blk
       [This metric roughly estimates fraction of cycles when the memory
        subsystem had loads blocked since they could not forward data from
        earlier (in program order) overlapping stores]
       [13 * cpu_core@LD_BLOCKS.STORE_FORWARD@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_fwd_blk > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(cpu_core@MEM_STORE_RETIRED.L2_HIT@ * 10 * (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) + (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) *
        min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO@)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_streaming_stores
       [This metric estimates how often CPU was stalled due to Streaming store
        memory accesses; Streaming store optimize out a read request required
        by RFO stores]
       [9 * cpu_core@OCR.STREAMING_WR.ANY_RESPONSE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_streaming_stores > 0.2 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_unknown_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        new branch address clears]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.UNKNOWN_BRANCH_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_unknown_branches > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_x87_use
       [This metric serves as an approximation of legacy x87 usage]
       [tma_retiring * cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.X87@ /
        cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD@]
       [tma_x87_use > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations > 0.6)]

tma_L5_group: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 5]
  tma_alu_op_utilization
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution ports for ALU operations]
       [(cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_0@ + cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_1@ +
        cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_5_11@ +
        cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_6@) / (5 * tma_info_core_core_clks)]
       [tma_alu_op_utilization > 0.4]
  tma_avx_assists
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops as a
        result of handing SSE to AVX* or AVX* to SSE transition Assists]
       [63 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.SSE_AVX_MIX@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_avx_assists > 0.1]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for (instruction)
        code accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for (instruction) code
        accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]
  tma_fp_assists
       [This metric roughly estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops
        as a result of handing Floating Point (FP) Assists]
       [30 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.FP@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fp_assists > 0.1]
  tma_fp_vector_128b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 128-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_128b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]
  tma_fp_vector_256b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 256-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_256b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]
  tma_load_op_utilization
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port for Load operations]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_2_3_10@ / (3 * tma_info_core_core_clks)]
       [tma_load_op_utilization > 0.6]
  tma_load_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the (first
        level) DTLB was missed by load accesses,that later on hit in
        second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [tma_dtlb_load - tma_load_stlb_miss]
       [tma_load_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1
        & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the Second-level
        TLB (STLB) was missed by load accesses,performing a hardware page walk]
       [cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1
        & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_mixing_vectors
       [This metric estimates penalty in terms of percentage of([SKL+]
        injected blend uops out of all Uops Issued -- the Count Domain; [ADL+]
        cycles)]
       [160 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.SSE_AVX_MIX@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mixing_vectors > 0.05]
  tma_page_faults
       [This metric roughly estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops
        as a result of handing Page Faults]
       [99 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.PAGE_FAULT@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_page_faults > 0.05]
  tma_store_op_utilization
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port for Store operations]
       [(cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_4_9@ +
        cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_7_8@) / (4 * tma_info_core_core_clks)]
       [tma_store_op_utilization > 0.6]
  tma_store_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the TLB was
        missed by store accesses,hitting in the second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [tma_dtlb_store - tma_store_stlb_miss]
       [tma_store_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound
        > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the STLB was missed
        by store accesses,performing a hardware page walk]
       [cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound
        > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]

tma_L6_group: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 6]
  tma_load_stlb_miss_1g
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 1 GB pages for data load accesses]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss_1g > 0.05 & (tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for data load
        accesses]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for data load accesses]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_port_0
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 0 ([SNB+] ALU; [HSW+] ALU and 2nd branch)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_0@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_0 > 0.6]
  tma_port_1
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 1 (ALU)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_1@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_1 > 0.6]
  tma_port_6
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 6 ([HSW+] Primary Branch and simple ALU)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_6@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_6 > 0.6]
  tma_store_stlb_miss_1g
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 1 GB pages for data store accesses]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss_1g > 0.05 & (tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for data store
        accesses]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@
        / (cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for data store accesses]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]

tma_alu_op_utilization_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_alu_op_utilization category]
  tma_port_0
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 0 ([SNB+] ALU; [HSW+] ALU and 2nd branch)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_0@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_0 > 0.6]
  tma_port_1
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 1 (ALU)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_1@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_1 > 0.6]
  tma_port_6
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 6 ([HSW+] Primary Branch and simple ALU)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_6@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_6 > 0.6]

tma_assists_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_assists category]
  tma_avx_assists
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops as a
        result of handing SSE to AVX* or AVX* to SSE transition Assists]
       [63 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.SSE_AVX_MIX@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_avx_assists > 0.1]
  tma_fp_assists
       [This metric roughly estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops
        as a result of handing Floating Point (FP) Assists]
       [30 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.FP@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fp_assists > 0.1]
  tma_page_faults
       [This metric roughly estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops
        as a result of handing Page Faults]
       [99 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.PAGE_FAULT@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_page_faults > 0.05]

tma_backend_bound_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_backend_bound category]
  tma_core_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where Core non-memory issues
        were of a bottleneck]
       [max(0,tma_backend_bound - tma_memory_bound)]
       [tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_memory_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the Memory subsystem within
        the Backend was a bottleneck]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-mem\-bound@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@) + 0 * tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_resource_bound
       [Counts the number of cycles the core is stalled due to a resource
        limitation]
       [tma_backend_bound - tma_core_bound]
       [tma_resource_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.1]

tma_bad_speculation_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_bad_speculation category]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-br\-mispredict@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@) + 0 * tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [max(0,tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts)]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]

tma_branch_mispredicts_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_branch_mispredicts category]
  tma_other_mispredicts
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        other cases of misprediction (non-retired x86 branches or other types)]
       [max(tma_branch_mispredicts * (1 -
        cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ /
        (cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEARS_COUNT@ - cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@)),
        0.0001)]
       [tma_other_mispredicts > 0.05 & (tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]

tma_branch_resteers_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_branch_resteers category]
  tma_clears_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Machine Clears]
       [(1 - tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation) *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_clears_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_mispredicts_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Branch Misprediction at execution stage]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mispredicts_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_unknown_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        new branch address clears]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.UNKNOWN_BRANCH_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_unknown_branches > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]

tma_code_stlb_miss_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_code_stlb_miss category]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for (instruction)
        code accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for (instruction) code
        accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]

tma_core_bound_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_core_bound category]
  tma_allocation_restriction
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not consumed by the backend
        due to certain allocation restrictions]
       [tma_core_bound]
       [tma_allocation_restriction > 0.1 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.1)]
  tma_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Divider unit was
        active]
       [cpu_core@ARITH.DIV_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_ports_utilization
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU performance was
        potentially limited due to Core computation issues (non
        divider-related)]
       [((tma_ports_utilized_0 * tma_info_thread_clks +
        (cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.1_PORTS_UTIL@ + tma_retiring *
        cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.2_3_PORTS_UTIL@)) / tma_info_thread_clks if
        cpu_core@ARITH.DIV_ACTIVE@ < cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_TOTAL@ -
        cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_LOADS@ else
        (cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.1_PORTS_UTIL@ + tma_retiring *
        cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.2_3_PORTS_UTIL@) / tma_info_thread_clks)]
       [tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_serializing_operation
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU issue-pipeline was
        stalled due to serializing operations]
       [cpu_core@RESOURCE_STALLS.SCOREBOARD@ / tma_info_thread_clks +
        tma_c02_wait]
       [tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]

tma_divider_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_divider category]
  tma_fp_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Floating-Point
        Divider unit was active]
       [cpu_core@ARITH.FPDIV_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fp_divider > 0.2 & (tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_int_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Integer Divider
        unit was active]
       [tma_divider - tma_fp_divider]
       [tma_int_divider > 0.2 & (tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

tma_dram_bound_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_dram_bound category]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=4@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

tma_dtlb_load_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_dtlb_load category]
  tma_load_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the (first
        level) DTLB was missed by load accesses,that later on hit in
        second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [tma_dtlb_load - tma_load_stlb_miss]
       [tma_load_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1
        & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the Second-level
        TLB (STLB) was missed by load accesses,performing a hardware page walk]
       [cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1
        & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]

tma_dtlb_store_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_dtlb_store category]
  tma_store_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the TLB was
        missed by store accesses,hitting in the second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [tma_dtlb_store - tma_store_stlb_miss]
       [tma_store_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound
        > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the STLB was missed
        by store accesses,performing a hardware page walk]
       [cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound
        > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]

tma_fetch_bandwidth_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_fetch_bandwidth category]
  tma_dsb
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to DSB (decoded uop cache) fetch pipeline]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_dsb > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_lsd
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to LSD (Loop Stream Detector) unit]
       [(cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_ACTIVE@ - cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_lsd > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_mite
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the MITE pipeline (the legacy decode pipeline)]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_ms
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit - see
        Microcode_Sequencer node for details]
       [max(cpu_core@IDQ.MS_CYCLES_ANY@,cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@)) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_ms > 0.05 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]

tma_fetch_latency_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_fetch_latency category]
  tma_branch_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks +
        tma_unknown_branches]
       [tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_dsb_switches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        switches from DSB to MITE pipelines]
       [cpu_core@DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dsb_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_TAG.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_lcp
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU was stalled due to
        Length Changing Prefixes (LCPs)]
       [cpu_core@DECODE.LCP@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lcp > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_ms_switches
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles when the CPU was stalled
        due to switches of uop delivery to the Microcode Sequencer (MS)]
       [3 * cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1\,edge@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ms_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]

tma_fp_arith_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_fp_arith category]
  tma_fp_scalar
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) scalar uops
        fraction the CPU has retired]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_scalar > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_fp_vector
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) vector uops
        fraction the CPU has retired aggregated across all vector widths]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.VECTOR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_x87_use
       [This metric serves as an approximation of legacy x87 usage]
       [tma_retiring * cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.X87@ /
        cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD@]
       [tma_x87_use > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations > 0.6)]

tma_fp_vector_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_fp_vector category]
  tma_fp_vector_128b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 128-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_128b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]
  tma_fp_vector_256b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 256-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_256b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]

tma_frontend_bound_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_frontend_bound category]
  tma_fetch_bandwidth
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend bandwidth issues]
       [max(0,tma_frontend_bound - tma_fetch_latency)]
       [tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend latency issues]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-fetch\-lat@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@) - cpu_core@INT_MISC.UOP_DROPPING@ /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_ifetch_bandwidth
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to frontend bandwidth restrictions due to decode,
        predecode,cisc,and other limitations]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.FRONTEND_BANDWIDTH@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_ifetch_latency
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to frontend latency restrictions due to icache misses,
        itlb misses,branch detection,and resteer limitations]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.FRONTEND_LATENCY@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_ifetch_latency > 0.15 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.2]

tma_heavy_operations_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_heavy_operations category]
  tma_few_uops_instructions
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        instructions that that are decoder into two or more uops]
       [max(0,tma_heavy_operations - tma_microcode_sequencer)]
       [tma_few_uops_instructions > 0.05 & tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_microcode_sequencer
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was retiring uops
        fetched by the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 & tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]

tma_icache_misses_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_icache_misses category]
  tma_code_l2_hit
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that hit in the L2 cache]
       [max(0,tma_icache_misses - tma_code_l2_miss)]
       [tma_code_l2_hit > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_l2_miss
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that miss in the L2 cache]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_CODE_RD@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_l2_miss > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]

tma_ifetch_bandwidth_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_ifetch_bandwidth category]
  tma_cisc
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to the microcode sequencer (MS)]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.CISC@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_cisc > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_decode
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to decode stalls]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.DECODE@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_decode > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_other_fb
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to other common frontend stalls not categorized]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.OTHER@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_other_fb > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.2)]
  tma_predecode
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to wrong predecodes]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.PREDECODE@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_predecode > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_bandwidth > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.2)]

tma_ifetch_latency_group: [Metrics contributing to tma_ifetch_latency category]
  tma_branch_detect
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to BACLEARS,which occurs when the Branch Target Buffer
        (BTB) prediction or lack thereof,was corrected by a later branch
        predictor in the frontend]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.BRANCH_DETECT@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_branch_detect > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_latency > 0.15 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_branch_resteer
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to BTCLEARS,which occurs when the Branch Target Buffer
        (BTB) predicts a taken branch]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.BRANCH_RESTEER@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_branch_resteer > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_latency > 0.15 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_icache_misses
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.ICACHE@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_latency > 0.15 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [Counts the number of issue slots that were not delivered by the
        frontend due to Instruction Table Lookaside Buffer (ITLB) misses]
       [cpu_atom@TOPDOWN_FE_BOUND.ITLB@ / (5 *
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@)]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_ifetch_latency > 0.15 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.2)]

tma_info_bottleneck_%_dtlb_miss_bound_cycles:

        data TLB miss]
       [100 * (cpu_atom@LD_HEAD.DTLB_MISS_AT_RET@ +
        cpu_atom@LD_HEAD.PGWALK_AT_RET@) / cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@]
